ints\n    if s.isdigit() or (s.startswith('-') and s[1:].isdigit()):\n        try:\n            return int(s)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n    try:\n        return float(s)\n    except Exception:\n        return s\n\n\ndef build_detail_sheet(wb: Workbook, title: str, csv_path: Path, gl_key: str, account_number: int):\n    headers, rows = read_csv(csv_path)\n\n    # Harbor-style base columns A..N are the monthly schedule columns.\n    # We insert a derived total column at O, then append the remaining metadata columns.\n    base_cols = [\n        'vendor',\n        'beginning_balance',\n        'jan_adds', 'jan_amortization', 'jan_ending_balance',\n        'feb_adds', 'feb_amortization', 'feb_ending_balance',\n        'mar_adds', 'mar_amortization', 'mar_ending_balance',\n        'apr_adds', 'apr_amortization', 'apr_ending_balance',\n    ]\n    trailing_cols = ['amortization_months', 'comments', 'account_number']\n\n    missing = [c for c in base_cols + trailing_cols if c not in headers]\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f\"{csv_path} missing columns: {missing}\")\n\n    sheet = wb.create_sheet(title)\n\n    # Basic header area (rows 1-5)\n    sheet['A1'] = title\n    sheet['A1'].font = Font(bold=True, size=14)\n    sheet['A1'].alignment = Alignment(horizontal='left')\n\n    sheet['A2'] = 'Account'\n    sheet['B2'] = account_number\n    sheet['A2'].font = Font(bold=True)\n\n    sheet['A3'] = 'Period'\n    sheet['B3'] = 'Jan–Apr 2025 (rollforward)'\n    sheet['A3'].font = Font(bold=True)\n\n    # Column headers row\n    HEADER_ROW = 5\n\n    # Map columns into Excel positions\n    # A..N = base_cols, O = derived total, P.. = trailing\n    excel_cols = base_cols + ['total_ending_balance'] + trailing_cols\n\n    for idx, key in enumerate(excel_cols, start=1):\n        cell = sheet.cell(row=HEADER_ROW, column=idx)\n        if key == 'total_ending_balance':\n            cell.value = 'total_ending_balance'\n        else:\n            cell.value = key\n        cell.font = Font(bold=True)\n        cell.fill = PatternFill('solid', fgColor='D9E1F2')\n        cell.alignment = Alignment(horizontal='center', wrap_text=True)\n\n    FIRST_DATA_ROW = 6\n    for r_i, r in enumerate(rows):\n        row_num = FIRST_DATA_ROW + r_i\n        # derived total column O = apr_ending_balance\n        total_col_idx = len(base_cols) + 1  # O in 1-indexed col numbering\n        apr_ending_col_letter = get_column_letter(base_cols.index('apr_ending_balance') + 1)\n        total_col_letter = get_column_letter(total_col_idx)\n        sheet.cell(row=row_num, column=total_col_idx, value=f\"={apr_ending_col_letter}{row_num}\")\n\n        # trailing metadata columns\n        for t_i, key in enumerate(trailing_cols, start=total_col_idx + 1):\n            if key == 'comments':\n                sheet.cell(row=row_num, column=t_i, value=r.get(key))\n            else:\n                sheet.cell(row=row_num, column=t_i, value=to_number(r.get(key)))\n\n    last_line_item_row = FIRST_DATA_ROW + len(rows) - 1\n\n    # Control rows\n    month_totals_row = last_line_item_row + 1\n    ending_balance_row = last_line_item_row + 2\n    variance_row = last_line_item_row + 3\n    gl_balance_row = last_line_item_row + 4\n\n    sheet.cell(row=month_totals_row, column=1, value='Month Totals').font = Font(bold=True)\n    sheet.cell(row=ending_balance_row, column=1, value='Ending Balance').font = Font(bold=True)\n    sheet.cell(row=variance_row, column=1, value='Variance').font = Font(bold=True)\n    sheet.cell(row=gl_balance_row, column=1, value='GL Balance').font = Font(bold=True)\n\n    #
